```python
def test_data_paths_layout_is_under_resource():
    paths = get_data_paths()
    assert paths.tag == "multi_domain"
    assert paths.raw_dir == PROJECT_ROOT / "resource" / "raw"
    assert paths.review_path("Video_Games").is_relative_to(paths.raw_dir)
    # The current layout stores product metadata separately from review dumps.
    # DataPaths prefers resource/metadata/meta_<domain>.jsonl when present and
    # falls back to the legacy resource/raw/meta_<domain>.jsonl.gz otherwise —
    # which layout exists depends on the local dataset, not the code.
    meta_preferred = PROJECT_ROOT / "resource" / "metadata" / "meta_Video_Games.jsonl"
    expected_meta = meta_preferred if meta_preferred.exists() else PROJECT_ROOT / "resource" / "raw" / "meta_Video_Games.jsonl.gz"
    assert paths.meta_path("Video_Games") == expected_meta
    assert paths.cleaned_path("Electronics") == PROJECT_ROOT / "resource/cleaned/Electronics/interactions.parquet"
    assert paths.filtered_path == PROJECT_ROOT / "resource/filtered/multi_domain/interactions.parquet"
    assert paths.mapped_dir == PROJECT_ROOT / "resource/mapped/multi_domain"
    assert paths.splits_dir == PROJECT_ROOT / "resource/splits/multi_domain"
    assert paths.split_path("train") == PROJECT_ROOT / "resource/splits/multi_domain/train.parquet"
```

```python
def test_config_cli_resolves_paths_from_datapaths_for_shell_scripts():
    def run(*args):
        out = subprocess.run(
            [sys.executable, "-m", "package.config", *args], capture_output=True, text=True, cwd=PROJECT_ROOT
        )
        return out.returncode, out.stdout.split()

    assert run("path:raw_dir") == (0, ["resource/raw"])
    assert run("path:filtered_path") == (0, ["resource/filtered/multi_domain/interactions.parquet"])
    assert run("--domain", "Video_Games", "path:splits_dir") == (0, ["resource/splits/Video_Games"])
    assert run("--domain", "Video_Games", "path:log_dir") == (0, ["experiments/logs/Video_Games"])
    assert run("path:experiments_dir") == (0, ["experiments"])
    assert run("path:review_path:Video_Games") == (0, [get_data_paths().review_path("Video_Games").relative_to(PROJECT_ROOT).as_posix()])
    assert run("path:meta_path:Video_Games") == (0, [get_data_paths().meta_path("Video_Games").relative_to(PROJECT_ROOT).as_posix()])
    assert run("path:cleaned_path:Electronics") == (0, ["resource/cleaned/Electronics/interactions.parquet"])
    assert run("--domain", "Electronics", "path:split_path:train") == (0, ["resource/splits/Electronics/train.parquet"])
    # shell and Python must agree: the CLI is just a view of DataPaths
    assert run("path:split_path:test") == (0, [get_data_paths().split_path("test").relative_to(PROJECT_ROOT).as_posix()])
    assert run("path:nope")[0] != 0
    assert run("path:cleaned_path")[0] != 0  # needs an argument
    assert run("path:raw_dir:oops")[0] != 0  # takes none
    assert run("path:split_path:validation_typo")[0] != 0
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/config/test_data_paths.py::test_review_resolver_supports_actual_layout_and_legacy_fallback`  (0.0098s)

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_review_resolver_supports_actual_layout_and_legacy_fallback(tmp_path):
    from dataclasses import replace
    paths = replace(get_data_paths(), raw_dir=tmp_path)
    assert paths.review_path("D") == tmp_path / "D.jsonl.gz"
    legacy = tmp_path / "D.jsonl.gz"
    legacy.write_bytes(b"test")
    assert paths.review_path("D") == legacy
    plain = tmp_path / "D.jsonl"
    plain.write_text("test")
    assert paths.review_path("D") == plain
    nested = tmp_path / "D" / "D.jsonl"
    nested.parent.mkdir()
    nested.write_text("test")
    assert paths.review_path("D") == nested
```

```python
def test_dependency_direction():
    offenders = []
    for path in _py_files(PACKAGE_ROOT):
        owner = _owner_package(_dotted_module(path))
        if owner is None:
            continue
        rel = path.relative_to(PROJECT_ROOT).as_posix()
        for dotted, lineno in _imported_modules(path):
            for forbidden in FORBIDDEN_IMPORTS[owner]:
                if _is_under(dotted, forbidden) and not any(
                    rel == file and _is_under(dotted, allowed) for file, allowed in ALLOWED_EXCEPTIONS
                ):
                    offenders.append(f"{rel}:{lineno}: {owner} must not import {dotted}")
    assert not offenders, "dependency-direction violations:\n" + "\n".join(sorted(set(offenders)))
```

```python
def test_allowed_exceptions_are_still_needed():
    """An exception that nothing uses any more must be deleted, so the list cannot rot."""
    used = set()
    for path in _py_files(PACKAGE_ROOT):
        rel = path.relative_to(PROJECT_ROOT).as_posix()
        for dotted, _ in _imported_modules(path):
            used.update((rel, allowed) for file, allowed in ALLOWED_EXCEPTIONS if rel == file and _is_under(dotted, allowed))
    assert used == ALLOWED_EXCEPTIONS
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/config/test_domain_registry.py::test_merge_domains_prevents_id_collision`  (0.0249s)

_Test QUAN TRỌNG NHẤT: cả 2 domain đều có raw parent_asin='ASIN_X'._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_merge_domains_prevents_id_collision(video_games_df, toys_games_df):
    """
    Test QUAN TRỌNG NHẤT: cả 2 domain đều có raw parent_asin='ASIN_X'.
    Nếu không namespace đúng, 2 item hoàn toàn khác nhau (1 video game,
    1 món đồ chơi) sẽ bị hệ thống hiểu nhầm là CÙNG 1 item.
    """
    tagged_vg = tag_domain(video_games_df, "Video_Games")
    tagged_tg = tag_domain(toys_games_df, "Toys_and_Games")
    merged = merge_domains([tagged_vg, tagged_tg])

    namespaced_ids = set(merged["parent_asin"])
    assert f"Video_Games{ITEM_ID_SEPARATOR}ASIN_X" in namespaced_ids
    assert f"Toys_and_Games{ITEM_ID_SEPARATOR}ASIN_X" in namespaced_ids
    # 2 item id namespaced phải KHÁC NHAU dù raw asin giống nhau
    assert f"Video_Games{ITEM_ID_SEPARATOR}ASIN_X" != f"Toys_and_Games{ITEM_ID_SEPARATOR}ASIN_X"
    # tổng số dòng đúng bằng tổng 2 domain (không mất/nhân đôi dữ liệu)
    assert len(merged) == len(video_games_df) + len(toys_games_df)
```

```python
    u1_rows = merged[merged["user_id"] == "u1"]
```
